```json
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 按 `optometry_id` 走 `idx_m10_optometry_device_optometry`；按 `parse_status` 走对应部分索引。
2. 返回列表不包含 `raw_payload`（体积大），详情需单独接口或 `include_payload=true`（限主管）。
3. 默认按 `received_at DESC`。

#### API-23 解析结果回写验光单

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/optometry/device/70031/write-back
```

```json
{
  "optometry_id": 5021,
  "target_stage": "RAW",
  "rows": [
    { "eye_side": "OD", "sphere": -2.50, "cylinder": -0.75, "axis": 175 },
    { "eye_side": "OS", "sphere": -2.75, "cylinder": -0.50, "axis": 5 }
  ],
  "double_check_by": null
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "written_row_count": 2,
    "written_at": "2026-10-31T09:42:10+08:00",
    "main_version": 11,
    "refraction_ids": [90330, 90331]
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `m10:optometry:update`；同步记录存在且 `parse_status IN ('PARSED','PARTIAL')`。
2. 校验目标阶段不可为 `ACCEPTED`（试戴必须人工完成），否则 `42200`。
3. 校验度数规则（0.25 步进、轴位规则）。
4. 事务内：写 `m10_optometry_refraction`（`is_device_imported = TRUE`）→ 更新同步记录 `written_to_optometry = TRUE`、`written_at`、`written_row_count`。
5. 若 `is_manual_fallback = TRUE`，`double_check_by` 必填，否则 `"1001011"`。
6. 主表 `version + 1`；写日志 `DEVICE_IMPORT`。

#### API-24 手工录入双人核对确认

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/optometry/device/70033/double-check
```

```json
{
  "double_check_by": 2005,
  "checked_refraction_ids": [90340, 90341],
  "remark": "与纸质小票逐项核对一致"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "sync_id": 70033,
    "is_double_checked": true,
    "double_check_by": 2005,
    "double_check_at": "2026-10-31T09:43:00+08:00",
    "refraction_updated": 2
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `m10:optometry:update`；同步记录 `is_manual_fallback = TRUE`。
2. 校验核对人 ≠ 录入人（`created_by`），否则 `"1001032"`（不允许自核）。
3. 更新同步记录 `is_double_checked = TRUE`、`double_check_by`、`double_check_at`。
4. 同步更新对应 `m10_optometry_refraction` 行的 `double_check_by`/`double_check_at`。
5. 写日志 `DOUBLE_CHECK`，`detail_json` 记录核对行数与备注。

#### API-25 提交主觉验光结果

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/optometry/5021/subjective
```

```json
{
  "steps": {
    "fogging": { "power": 0.75, "minutes": 10 },
    "red_green": { "result": "GREEN_CLEAR", "added_sphere": -0.25 },
    "jcc_axis": { "rounds": 4, "final_axis": 180 },
    "jcc_cylinder": { "rounds": 4, "final_cylinder": -0.50 },
    "binocular_balance": { "method": "ALTERNATE_COVER", "adjust": { "OS": -0.25 } }
  },
  "eyes": [
    { "eye_side": "OD", "sphere": -1.75, "cylinder": -0.50, "axis": 180, "va_corrected": 1.00 },
    { "eye_side": "OS", "sphere": -2.00, "cylinder": -0.25, "axis": 10, "va_corrected": 0.90 }
  ],
  "double_check_by": 2005
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "refraction_ids": [90350, 90351],
    "stage": "FINAL",
    "calc": { "sphere_equiv_od": -2.00, "sphere_equiv_os": -2.13, "anisometropia": 0.13 },
    "suggest_trial": true,
    "main_version": 12
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验状态、编辑锁、度数规则。
2. 校验步骤完整性：`fogging`/`red_green`/`jcc_axis`/`jcc_cylinder` 四项齐全，否则 `42200`（未完成雾视不可直接精调）。
3. 写入两行 `refraction_stage='FINAL'`（`measure_method='PHOROPTER'`，`is_subjective=TRUE`）；若已存在 FINAL 则覆盖（同 `stage_seq` 更新）。
4. 重算派生指标；返回 `suggest_trial=true` 提示进入试戴。
5. 主表 `version + 1`；写日志 `UPDATE`。

#### API-26 提交试戴结果

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/optometry/5021/trial
```

```json
{
  "eyes": [
    { "eye_side": "OD", "sphere": -1.75, "cylinder": -0.50, "axis": 180, "va_corrected": 1.00, "is_accepted": true },
    { "eye_side": "OS", "sphere": -2.00, "cylinder": -0.25, "axis": 10, "va_corrected": 0.90, "is_accepted": true }
  ],
  "trial_minutes": 15,
  "comfort": "SLIGHT_DIZZY",
  "feedback": "看远清楚，下楼梯略有不稳感",
  "handling": "ACCEPT",
  "reject_reason": null
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "refraction_ids": [90360, 90361],
    "stage": "ACCEPTED",
    "all_accepted": true,
    "main_version": 13
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验存在双眼 FINAL 数据（否则 `"1001009"`），以及校验锁与度数规则。
2. 校验 ACCEPTED 阶段必须带 `va_corrected`（`"1001009"`）。
3. `is_accepted = FALSE` 时必须填 `reject_reason`（`40001`）。
4. 写两行 `refraction_stage='ACCEPTED'`（`measure_method='TRIAL_LENS'`）。
5. `handling` 支持 `ACCEPT`/`REDUCE_SPHERE`/`REDUCE_CYL`/`REJECT`；减配场景返回 `suggestion`（见 API-27）。
6. 主表 `version + 1`；写日志 `UPDATE`。

#### API-27 试戴建议（减配方案）

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/optometry/5021/trial/suggestion?comfort=SEVERE_UNCOMFORT
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "current": [
      { "eye_side": "OD", "sphere": -1.75, "cylinder": -0.50, "axis": 180 },
      { "eye_side": "OS", "sphere": -2.00, "cylinder": -0.25, "axis": 10 }
    ],
    "suggestions": [
      {
        "type": "REDUCE_SPHERE",
        "label": "球镜各减 0.25D",
        "delta": -0.25,
        "eyes": [
          { "eye_side": "OD", "sphere": -1.50, "cylinder": -0.50, "axis": 180 },
          { "eye_side": "OS", "sphere": -1.75, "cylinder": -0.25, "axis": 10 }
        ],
        "rationale": "首次配镜、散瞳后度数，减配可提高适应性"
      },
      {
        "type": "REDUCE_CYL",
        "label": "柱镜各减 0.25D",
        "delta": -0.25,
        "eyes": [
          { "eye_side": "OD", "sphere": -1.75, "cylinder": -0.25, "axis": 180 },
          { "eye_side": "OS", "sphere": -2.00, "cylinder": 0.0, "axis": null }
        ],
        "rationale": "低度散光可先不矫足，观察适应性"
      }
    ],
    "notes": ["减配后需重新试戴并记录矫正视力", "儿童首配不宜过度减配，以免影响视觉发育"]
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 读取当前 FINAL/ACCEPTED 度数。
2. 按不适程度生成候选方案：`SLIGHT_DIZZY` → 仅球镜减 0.25；`SEVERE_UNCOMFORT` → 球镜与柱镜各一档。
3. 减配规则：球镜每次 0.25D；柱镜减到 0 时轴位置空（由前端/后端统一处理）。
4. 首次配镜、散瞳后、屈光参差 >2.00D 时附 `rationale` 说明。
5. 只返回建议，不写库；用户确认后调 API-26 落库。

#### API-28 重算派生指标

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/optometry/5021/calculate
```

```json
{
  "force": true
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "sphere_equiv_od": -2.00,
    "sphere_equiv_os": -2.13,
    "anisometropia": 0.13,
    "max_cylinder": 0.50,
    "is_amblyopia_suspect": true,
    "amblyopia_reasons": ["OS 矫正视力 0.90 低于 6 岁及以上筛查线 0.80"],
    "is_progression_alert": false,
    "progression_delta": null,
    "previous_optometry_no": null,
    "main_version": 14
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验状态与权限（`m10:optometry:update`）。
2. 执行 `OptometryCalcService`：取 FINAL（无则 ACCEPTED）→ 计算 SE（0.25 舍入）→ 屈光参差 → 最大柱镜。
3. 年进展：查 `previous_optometry_no`（无则按患者取最近一次已签发单）；间隔 ≥90 天才计算。
4. 弱视判定：按 AMB-01~AMB-05 规则逐条评估，返回命中原因。
5. 写回主表并 `version + 1`；`force=false` 且结果无变化时不写库。
6. 写日志 `UPDATE`，`detail_json` 记录计算前后的标记变化。

#### API-29 批量保存视功能检查

**请求**

```
PUT /api/v1/m10/optometry/5021/visual-funcs
```

```json
{
  "items": [
    { "eye_side": "OU", "item_code": "WORTH4", "item_category": "SENSORY", "test_condition": "FAR", "value_text": "NORMAL" },
    { "eye_side": "OU", "item_code": "STEREOPSIS", "item_category": "SENSORY", "test_condition": "NEAR", "test_distance_cm": 40, "value_num": 60, "value_unit": "SEC_OF_ARC" },
    { "eye_side": "OU", "item_code": "FUSION_RANGE", "item_category": "MOTOR", "test_condition": "NEAR", "test_distance_cm": 40, "value_unit": "PD", "value_json": { "bo_blur": 9, "bo_break": 15, "bo_recovery": 11, "bi_blur": 7, "bi_break": 12, "bi_recovery": 9 } },
    { "eye_side": "OU", "item_code": "PHORIA_NEAR", "item_category": "MOTOR", "test_condition": "NEAR", "test_distance_cm": 40, "value_num": -6, "value_unit": "PD" }
  ],
  "replace_all": false
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "saved_count": 4,
    "abnormal_count": 0,
    "abnormal_items": [],
    "main_version": 15
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验状态、编辑锁；每行至少一个取值字段（`40001`）。
2. 校验 `test_condition='NEAR'` 必填 `test_distance_cm`（`40001`）。
3. 校验取值合理性（`"1001024"`）：立体视 >0、AC/A 0~30、AMP >0、融合范围六值齐全且 `模糊 ≤ 破裂`、`恢复 ≤ 破裂`。
4. 判定 `is_abnormal` 与 `abnormal_level`，生成 `abnormal_hint`（规则见 8.5）。
5. `replace_all=true` 先软删全部行；否则按 `(eye_side, item_code, test_condition, test_seq)` 差量同步。
6. 主表 `version + 1`；写日志 `UPDATE`。

#### API-30 查询视功能检查

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/optometry/5021/visual-funcs?item_category=SENSORY&abnormal_only=false
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {
        "id": 80011,
        "eye_side": "OU",
        "item_code": "STEREOPSIS",
        "item_name": "立体视",
        "item_category": "SENSORY",
        "test_condition": "NEAR",
        "test_distance_cm": 40,
        "value_num": 60,
        "value_unit": "SEC_OF_ARC",
        "value_normal": "≤60 秒弧",
        "is_abnormal": false,
        "abnormal_level": null,
        "abnormal_hint": null,
        "is_reliable": true
      }
    ],
    "abnormal_summary": { "SENSORY": 0, "MOTOR": 0, "ACCOMMODATION": 1, "OTHER": 0 }
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 走 `idx_m10_optometry_vf_main`；`abnormal_only=true` 时走 `idx_m10_optometry_vf_abnormal`。
2. `value_normal` 由服务层按检查项与年龄上下文填充（如 AMP 按 Hofstetter 公式）。
3. `abnormal_summary` 按分类统计异常项数，供页面顶部徽章显示。

#### API-31 视功能正常参考值

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/optometry/visual-func/standards?age=8&refraction_stage=FINAL
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      { "item_code": "WORTH4", "item_name": "Worth 4 点", "unit": "NONE", "normal_text": "远/近均见 4 点" },
      { "item_code": "STEREOPSIS", "item_name": "立体视", "unit": "SEC_OF_ARC", "normal_text": "≤60 秒弧" },
      { "item_code": "AMP", "item_name": "调节幅度", "unit": "D", "normal_text": "≥13.00 D（按年龄 8 岁计算：15 − 0.25 × 8）" },
      { "item_code": "AC_A", "item_name": "AC/A", "unit": "PD", "normal_text": "3~5 △/D" },
      { "item_code": "PHORIA_FAR", "item_name": "远隐斜", "unit": "PD", "normal_text": "1△ 外隐斜 ~ 3△ 内隐斜" },
      { "item_code": "PHORIA_NEAR", "item_name": "近隐斜", "unit": "PD", "normal_text": "0~6△ 外隐斜" },
      { "item_code": "NPC", "item_name": "集合近点", "unit": "MM", "normal_text": "≤60 mm" }
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 登录即可；按 `age` 动态计算 AMP 等与年龄相关的阈值（`15 − 0.25 × age`，下限 3.00D）。
2. 返回 13 类检查项的参考值（完整表见附录 D）。
3. 结果缓存 Redis `m10:optometry:standards:{age}` TTL 3600s。

#### API-32 签发前检查清单

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/optometry/5021/sign-check
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "can_sign": false,
    "checks": [
      { "code": "SUBJECT", "label": "患者标识齐全", "result": "PASS", "detail": "patient_id 10021" },
      { "code": "TYPE_PURPOSE", "label": "验光类型与目的已选", "result": "PASS", "detail": "CYCLOPLEGIC / PRESCRIPTION" },
      { "code": "FINAL_BOTH_EYES", "label": "双眼 FINAL 屈光数据", "result": "PASS", "detail": "OD -1.75/-0.50×180；OS -2.00/-0.25×10" },
      { "code": "ACCEPTED_BOTH_EYES", "label": "双眼 ACCEPTED 数据", "result": "PASS", "detail": "已接受" },
      { "code": "VA_RECORDED", "label": "远/近视力已录入", "result": "PASS", "detail": "OD 1.00/1.00；OS 0.90/0.90" },
      { "code": "PD_RECORDED", "label": "瞳距已录入", "result": "PASS", "detail": "远 54.50mm" },
      { "code": "CYCLO_COMPLIANT", "label": "散瞳合规", "result": "PASS", "detail": "8 岁首诊已散瞳" },
      { "code": "CYCLO_WAIT", "label": "散瞳等待充足", "result": "WARN", "detail": "末次滴药 09:02 → 开始 09:25（23 分钟，建议 ≥30）" },
      { "code": "DOUBLE_CHECK", "label": "手工录入已双人核对", "result": "PASS", "detail": "4 行，核对人 张主任" },
      { "code": "VISUAL_FUNC", "label": "视功能已录入", "result": "PASS", "detail": "12 项（1 项轻度异常）" },
      { "code": "REVIEWER", "label": "复核人已指定", "result": "PASS", "detail": "张主任" }
    ],
    "warnings": ["散瞳等待 23 分钟 < 建议 30 分钟"],
    "infos": ["弱视疑似：OS 矫正视力 0.90 < 0.80 线，需医师复核"],
    "blockers": []
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `m10:optometry:sign`；单必须为 `DRAFT` 且属于当前用户（或有主管权限）。
2. 逐项执行 11 条检查（见 8.4 签发校验规则）。
3. `result` 三态：`PASS` / `WARN`（可签发需确认）/ `FAIL`（阻断，进 `blockers`）。
4. `can_sign = blockers.is_empty()`。
5. 返回 `warnings` 与 `infos` 供前端展示确认勾选框。

#### API-33 签发验光单

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/optometry/5021/sign
Idempotency-Key: opto-sign-5021
```

```json
{
  "version": 15,
  "optometrist_id": 2001,
  "reviewer_id": 2005,
  "e_signature_id": "CA-2026-1031-0012345",
  "signature_pin": "A8x29k",
  "accept_warnings": true,
  "print_after_sign": false
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 5021,
    "optometry_no": "YG202610310042",
    "status": "SIGNED",
    "signed_at": "2026-10-31T09:52:10+08:00",
    "signed_by": 2001,
    "signed_by_name": "李视光",
    "e_signature_id": "CA-2026-1031-0012345",
    "e_signature_hash": "a3f1c8e2b7d94f05e6a2c8d1f4b7e093",
    "patient_viewable": true,
    "version": 16
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `m10:optometry:sign` 与 `Idempotency-Key`（命中直接返回原结果）。
2. 校验状态为 `DRAFT`（`"1001002"`）；已作废 `"1001026"`；草稿过期 `"1001040"`。
3. 校验 `version` 乐观锁（`40900`）。
4. 校验签发人资质：调 M01-02 `staff/{id}/brief` 检查 `is_optometrist = TRUE`，否则 `"1001020"`。
5. 校验非试戴配镜目的时 `ACCEPTED` 必须存在且 `is_accepted = TRUE`，否则 `"1001025"`。
6. 执行签发前全部检查，存在 `FAIL` 返回 `"1001007"`（带 `blockers` 列表）；`WARN` 需 `accept_warnings=true`（否则 `"1001008"`/`"1001010"` 等）。
7. 生成验光单号：`INCR m10:code:YG:{yyyyMMdd}` → `YG + yyyyMMdd + %04d`；唯一冲突重试 3 次。
8. 计算签名摘要：主表关键字段 + 全部屈光明细 + 全部视力 + 全部视功能序列化后 SM3。
9. 调 M02 电子签名服务 `POST /internal/v1/m02/sign/seal`；失败返回 `"1001019"` 且**不落号**（事务回滚，号段可回收）。
10. 事务内：更新 `status='SIGNED'`、`optometry_no`、`signed_at`、`signed_by`、`e_signature_id`、`e_signature_hash`、`patient_viewable=TRUE`、`version+1`、`end_at`（若空则填当前）。
11. 写日志 `SIGN`（含 `signature_hash`）；发事件 `m10.optometry.signed`；失效相关缓存；释放编辑锁。
12. 若 `print_after_sign`，同步触发打印流程（API-34 内部调用）。

#### API-34 打印验光单

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/optometry/5021/print
```

```json
{
  "template_code": "YG_A5_V1",
  "copies": 1,
  "with_suggestion": true
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "optometry_no": "YG202610310042",
    "printed_count": 1,
    "printed_at": "2026-10-31T09:53:20+08:00",
    "pdf_url": "/upload/m10/optometry/YG202610310042_v1.pdf",
    "verify_hash": "a3f1c8e2b7d94f05e6a2c8d1f4b7e093"
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `m10:optometry:print`；单必须 `SIGNED` 或 `ARCHIVED`（`DRAFT` 返回 `"1001007"`，`VOID` 返回 `"1001013"`）。
2. **验签**：重新计算摘要与 `e_signature_hash` 比对，不一致返回 `"1001023"` 并告警。
3. 渲染 PDF（A5 版式，含医院名称、患者信息、双眼度数表、瞳距、视功能摘要、验光师/复核人、盖章位、二维码）。
4. `printed_count + 1`、`last_printed_at`、`last_printed_by`；写日志 `PRINT`（含 `signature_hash`）。
5. 返回 PDF 地址（对象存储）供前端下载或直接送打印。
6. 打印次数无上限但每次留痕；重复打印在 PDF 上加「补打 第 N 次」水印。

#### API-35 作废验光单

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/optometry/5021/void
Idempotency-Key: opto-void-5021
```

```json
{
  "void_reason": "散瞳后复验度数与首次不符，需重开新单",
  "reopen": false,
  "notify_downstream": true
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 5021,
    "optometry_no": "YG202610310042",
    "status": "VOID",
    "voided_at": "2026-10-31T10:20:00+08:00",
    "voided_by": 2005,
    "notified": ["M10-02", "M16-04"],
    "reopen_id": null
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `m10:optometry:void`（主管级）；状态必须 `SIGNED`（`ARCHIVED` 返回 `"1001030"`）。
2. 校验 `void_reason` 长度 ≥10 字（`"1001013"`）。
3. 校验无有效配镜处方引用：调 M10-02 `GET /internal/v1/m10/prescriptions/valid/by-optometry/{optometry_no}`（若 M10-02 未实现则查本地 `prescription_suggestion_json` 的反向引用字段）→ 存在则返回 `"1001014"` 并提示先作废处方。
4. 事务内：`status='VOID'`、`void_reason`、`voided_by`、`voided_at`、`patient_viewable=FALSE`、`version+1`。
5. 写日志 `VOID`（含 `signature_hash`）；发事件 `m10.optometry.voided`。
6. `reopen=true` 时同事务调用重开逻辑（API-36 内部方法）生成新草稿并返回 `reopen_id`。
7. 作废后重算后续单的 `progression_delta`（因基线变了），异步任务处理。

#### API-36 作废重开

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/optometry/5021/reopen
Idempotency-Key: opto-reopen-5021
```

```json
{
  "void_reason": "散瞳后复验度数与首次不符，需重开新单",
  "copy_patient_info": true,
  "copy_cycloplegic_info": false
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "source_optometry_no": "YG202610310042",
    "new_optometry_id": 5062,
    "new_status": "DRAFT",
    "regen_from_id": 5021,
    "copied_refraction_rows": 0,
    "message": "已生成新草稿，请重新录入屈光数据"
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `m10:optometry:create` 与 `m10:optometry:void`；源单必须 `SIGNED`（`ARCHIVED` 返回 `"1001030"`）。
2. 先执行作废（复用 API-35 逻辑），再新建草稿。
3. 复制内容：患者信息（姓名/性别/生日/就诊号）必复制；散瞳信息与屈光数据可选（默认不复制度数，避免把错误数据带过去）。
4. 新单 `regen_from_id = 源单 id`，`previous_optometry_no = 源单号`。
5. 写日志 `REGEN`（源单与新单各一条）。
6. 返回新草稿 ID，前端跳转 P03。

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/optometry?keyword=王小明&status=SIGNED&optometry_type=CYCLOPLEGIC
    &date_from=2026-01-01&date_to=2026-10-31&is_amblyopia_suspect=true&page=1&page_size=20
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {
        "id": 5021,
        "optometry_no": "YG202610310042",
        "patient_id": 10021,
        "patient_name": "王小明",
        "gender": "M",
        "age": 8,
        "age_group": "TEEN",
        "optometry_type": "CYCLOPLEGIC",
        "optometry_purpose": "PRESCRIPTION",
        "is_cycloplegic": true,
        "status": "SIGNED",
        "exam_date": "2026-10-31",
        "optometrist_id": 2001,
        "optometrist_name": "李视光",
        "sphere_equiv_od": -2.00,
        "sphere_equiv_os": -2.13,
        "is_amblyopia_suspect": true,
        "is_progression_alert": false,
        "signed_at": "2026-10-31T09:52:10+08:00"
      }
    ],
    "total": 128,
    "page": 1,
    "page_size": 20,
    "pages": 7
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `m10:optometry:view` 与数据权限（验光师本人 + 本科室；主管/质控全科；患者仅本人已签发）。
2. 组合查询：`keyword` 用 `pg_trgm` 模糊匹配 `patient_name` 或精确匹配 `optometry_no`/`phone`/`visit_no`；`status`/`optometry_type` 多值 `IN`；日期区间走 `idx_m10_optometry_date`。
3. `is_amblyopia_suspect`/`is_progression_alert` 走对应部分索引。
4. 日期跨度 >731 天返回 `40001`。
5. 排序：`exam_date DESC, id DESC`；`page_size` 上限 200。

#### API-38 患者验光历史趋势

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/optometry/5021/history-trend?limit=5
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "points": [
      { "optometry_no": "YG202403150021", "exam_date": "2024-03-15", "sphere_equiv_od": -2.00, "sphere_equiv_os": -2.25, "delta_yearly": null },
      { "optometry_no": "YG202410200033", "exam_date": "2024-10-20", "sphere_equiv_od": -2.50, "sphere_equiv_os": -2.75, "delta_yearly": -0.86 },
      { "optometry_no": "YG202505120018", "exam_date": "2025-05-12", "sphere_equiv_od": -3.00, "sphere_equiv_os": -3.25, "delta_yearly": -0.86 },
      { "optometry_no": "YG202602080044", "exam_date": "2026-02-08", "sphere_equiv_od": -3.25, "sphere_equiv_os": -3.50, "delta_yearly": -0.34 },
      { "optometry_no": "YG202610310042", "exam_date": "2026-10-31", "sphere_equiv_od": -2.00, "sphere_equiv_os": -2.13, "delta_yearly": 2.10 }
    ],
    "alert_points": ["YG202505120018"],
    "trend_note": "2026-10-31 为散瞳后验光结果，与既往未散瞳数据不可直接比较"
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验权限；按 `patient_id` 走 `idx_m10_optometry_patient` 取最近 `limit` 张已签发单。
2. `delta_yearly` = 与上一点的 SE 差 ÷ 间隔年数（间隔 <90 天为 null）。
3. `alert_points` 为 `is_progression_alert = TRUE` 的单。
4. **一致性提示**：若相邻两次验光一次散瞳一次未散瞳，`trend_note` 明确提示不可直接比较（避免误读）。
5. 若患者有 `merge_from_patient_id`，同时聚合合并前的历史。

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/optometry/5021/logs?limit=50
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {
        "id": 301,
        "action": "SIGN",
        "action_result": "SUCCESS",

      },
      {
```

3. 返回动作、结果、操作人、角色、变更字段、摘要（不返回 `old_value`/`new_value` 全文，避免体量大；需要时用 `include_diff=true`，限主管）。
4. 日志只增不改，结果为只读视图。

#### API-40 患者全部验光时间线

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/optometry/patient/10021/timeline?date_from=2024-01-01&page=1&page_size=20
```

```json
{

  },

}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 按 `patient_id` 取全部历史验光单（含 `VOID`，但标注状态）。
2. `linked_rx_count` 与 `linked_order_count` 由 M10-02 / M16-04 事件回写的反向引用计数（本表不存明细，仅计数缓存）。
3. 供 P08 患者档案视图与 M07 医生工作站「验光史」使用。

**响应**

```json
{

    },

      {
        "key": "2001",
        "label": "李视光",
        "total_count": 386,
        "signed_count": 380,
        "cycloplegic_count": 168,
        "child_count": 92,
        "avg_duration_minutes": 15.2,
        "voided_rate": 0.0080,
        "double_check_rate": 1.0,
        "rx_conversion_rate": 0.7250
      }
    ],
    "quality_metrics": [
      { "code": "CHILD_CYCLO_RATE", "label": "6 岁以下首诊散瞳率", "value": 0.963, "target": 0.95, "trend": [0.941, 0.950, 0.958, 0.960, 0.961, 0.963] },
      { "code": "TEEN_CYCLO_SUGGEST_RATE", "label": "6~17 岁首诊散瞳建议率", "value": 0.928, "target": 0.90, "trend": [0.882, 0.895, 0.901, 0.910, 0.920, 0.928] },
      { "code": "DEVICE_IMPORT_RATE", "label": "设备直连导入成功率", "value": 0.986, "target": 0.98, "trend": [0.979, 0.981, 0.983, 0.984, 0.985, 0.986] }
    ],

}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `m10:optometry:stat`；病区/科室维度按数据权限过滤。
2. 优先读 `m10_optometry_stat` 预聚合；当日数据从明细实时汇总并与预聚合合并（`is_realtime=true`）。
3. `group_by` 支持 `OPTOMETRIST`/`DEPT`/`TYPE`/`HOUR`。
4. `quality_metrics` 的 `trend` 取近 6 个月同口径值。
5. 周期跨度 >366 天时返回 `40001`（引导走月报）。

#### API-43 统计导出


```json
  "date_from": "2026-10-01",
  "date_to": "2026-10-31",
  "group_by": "OPTOMETRIST",
  "format": "xlsx",

}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "task_id": "exp_20261031_0007",
    "status": "QUEUED",
    "estimated_rows": 12,

  },

}
```

1. 校验 `m10:optometry:export`。
2. 预估行数 >5,000 返回 `"1001022"`（统计类一般远低于此）。
3. 提交 Celery 任务 `export_optometry_statistics`，返回任务号。
4. 完成后写对象存储并通过 M03-05 消息 + 站内通知推送下载链接。
5. 导出行为写日志 `EXPORT`。

```json
{
```

2. 返回全局 + 指定科室的行；`effective` 为按「科室 > 全局 > 默认」优先级计算后的实际生效值。
3. 结果缓存 Redis Hash `m10:optometry:config` TTL 600s。

#### API-45 修改验光参数配置

**请求**

```
PUT /api/v1/m10/optometry/config
```

```json
{
  "items": [
    { "param_code": "M10.OPTOMETRY.CYCLO_SUGGEST_AGE", "scope": "DEPT", "scope_id": 31, "param_value": "16" },
    { "param_code": "M10.OPTOMETRY.PROGRESSION_THRESHOLD", "scope": "GLOBAL", "scope_id": null, "param_value": "0.75" }
  ]
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "updated": 2,
    "skipped_readonly": [],
    "cache_invalidated": true
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `m10:optometry:config`；逐项校验参数存在、类型匹配、值在 `min_value`~`max_value` 区间。
2. 若 `is_editable = FALSE` 拒绝修改并返回 `"1001039"`（合规参数只能发版调整）。
3. 更新或插入（DEPT 级不存在则新增）；`version + 1`。
4. `DEL m10:optometry:config` 并 `INCR m10:version:optometry` 广播失效。
5. 写日志 `UPDATE`（`target_table='m10_optometry_config'`）。

#### API-46 P-01 按验光单号取验光结果（内部契约）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/optometry/{optometry_no}
Header: X-Internal-Token: <service-token>
```

示例：

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/optometry/YG202610310042?include_visual_func=false
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "optometry_no": "YG202610310042",
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "patient_name": "王小明",
    "gender": "M",
    "age": 8,
    "visit_no": "V2026103100123",
    "optometry_type": "CYCLOPLEGIC",
    "optometry_purpose": "PRESCRIPTION",
    "is_cycloplegic": true,
    "status": "SIGNED",
    "exam_date": "2026-10-31",
    "signed_at": "2026-10-31T09:52:10+08:00",
    "signed_by": 2001,
    "optometrist_name": "李视光",
    "eyes": [
      {
        "eye_side": "OD",
        "sphere": -1.75,
        "cylinder": -0.50,
        "axis": 180,
        "add_power": null,
        "prism_h": 0,
        "prism_v": 0,
        "va": 1.00,
        "sphere_equivalent": -2.00,
        "source_stage": "ACCEPTED"
      },
      {
        "eye_side": "OS",
        "sphere": -2.00,
        "cylinder": -0.25,
        "axis": 10,
        "add_power": null,
        "prism_h": 0,
        "prism_v": 0,
        "va": 0.90,
        "sphere_equivalent": -2.13,
        "source_stage": "ACCEPTED"
      }
    ],
    "pd_binocular": 54.50,
    "pd_right": 27.25,
    "pd_left": 27.25,
    "pd_near_binocular": 52.00,
    "ph_od": 22.0,
    "ph_os": 22.5,
    "is_amblyopia_suspect": true,
    "is_progression_alert": false,
    "e_signature_hash": "a3f1c8e2b7d94f05e6a2c8d1f4b7e093",
    "cached": false
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. **不校验用户权限，改为校验 `X-Internal-Token`**（服务间令牌，M02 颁发）；无效返回 `40300`。
2. 按 `optometry_no` 查主表（`uk_m10_optometry_no`）。
3. 状态校验：`DRAFT` 返回 `"1001002"`（未签发不可用于开单）；`VOID` 返回 `"1001001"`（视为不存在）；记录不存在返回 `"1001001"`。
4. 取双眼度数：优先 `ACCEPTED`，无则 `FINAL`，均无返回 `"1001009"`。`source_stage` 返回实际取数阶段，让调用方知道用的是哪一个。
5. 取瞳距/瞳高：`check_kind='PD'` 与 `'PH'` 行。
6. `include_visual_func=true` 时附带视功能摘要（默认 false，减小载荷）。
7. 缓存 Redis `m10:optometry:p01:{optometry_no}` TTL 300s（签发后内容不可变，可安全缓存）。
8. 记录访问日志 `PATIENT_VIEW` 之外的内部访问计数（用于 P-01 调用量监控）。

**调用方与 SLA：**

| 调用方 | 用途 | SLA |
|---|---|---|
| M16-04 | U-04：创建销售单时校验验光数据 | P95 ≤ 100ms |
| M07 | 医生工作站查看验光结果 | P95 ≤ 100ms |
| M11 | 屈光手术术前评估参考 | P95 ≤ 100ms |

#### API-47 P-02 患者最近一次验光（内部契约）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/optometry/by-patient/{patient_id}/latest
Header: X-Internal-Token: <service-token>
```

示例：

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/optometry/by-patient/10021/latest?max_age_days=365
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "found": true,
    "optometry_no": "YG202610310042",
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "patient_name": "王小明",
    "age": 8,
    "exam_date": "2026-10-31",
    "signed_at": "2026-10-31T09:52:10+08:00",
    "is_cycloplegic": true,
    "age_days": 0,
    "is_expired": false,
    "eyes": [
      { "eye_side": "OD", "sphere": -1.75, "cylinder": -0.50, "axis": 180, "va": 1.00, "sphere_equivalent": -2.00 },
      { "eye_side": "OS", "sphere": -2.00, "cylinder": -0.25, "axis": 10, "va": 0.90, "sphere_equivalent": -2.13 }
    ],
    "pd_binocular": 54.50,
    "is_amblyopia_suspect": true,
    "is_progression_alert": false,
    "optometrist_name": "李视光"
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `X-Internal-Token`。
2. 走**主治索引** `idx_m10_optometry_latest`（`patient_id` + `signed_at DESC`，条件 `status='SIGNED'`），`LIMIT 1`。
3. 无记录返回 `"1001038"`（404），但**返回体仍为 `found=false`**（便于调用方判断而非抛异常）。
4. `age_days` = 今天 − `exam_date`；`is_expired` = `age_days > max_age_days`（默认 365，可传参；配镜一般要求 12 个月内）。
5. `patient_id` 若为合并后的 ID，自动包含合并前记录（`merge_from_patient_id` 反向查）。
6. 缓存 Redis `m10:optometry:p02:{patient_id}` TTL 120s；签发/作废时失效。

**调用方与 SLA：**

| 调用方 | 用途 | SLA |
|---|---|---|
| M07 | 医生工作站打开患者详情时展示最近验光 | P95 ≤ 100ms |
| M11 | 屈光手术术前参考 | P95 ≤ 100ms |
| M16-04 | 开单时预填度数 | P95 ≤ 100ms |

#### API-48 P-03 由验光单生成处方建议（内部契约）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/optometry/{optometry_no}/prescription-suggestion
Header: X-Internal-Token: <service-token>
```

示例：

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/optometry/YG202610310042/prescription-suggestion?lens_preference=DEFOCUS
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "optometry_no": "YG202610310042",
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "patient_name": "王小明",
    "age": 8,
    "is_cycloplegic": true,
    "recommendation_level": "CAUTION",
    "suggested_lens_type": "DEFOCUS",
    "eyes": [
      {
        "eye_side": "OD",
        "sphere": -1.75,
        "cylinder": -0.50,
        "axis": 180,
        "add_power": null,
        "prism_h": 0,
        "prism_v": 0,
        "prism_base_h": null,
        "prism_base_v": null,
        "va": 1.00,
        "source_stage": "ACCEPTED",
        "adjusted": false,
        "adjust_note": null,
        "power_text": "-1.75DS -0.50DC×180"
      },
      {
        "eye_side": "OS",
        "sphere": -2.00,
        "cylinder": -0.25,
        "axis": 10,
        "add_power": null,
        "prism_h": 0,
        "prism_v": 0,
        "prism_base_h": null,
        "prism_base_v": null,
        "va": 0.90,
        "source_stage": "ACCEPTED",
        "adjusted": false,
        "adjust_note": null,
        "power_text": "-2.00DS -0.25DC×10"
      }
    ],
    "pd_binocular": 54.50,
    "pd_right": 27.25,
    "pd_left": 27.25,
    "pd_near_binocular": 52.00,
    "ph_od": 22.0,
    "ph_os": 22.5,
    "warnings": [
      "OS 矫正视力 0.90，低于 6 岁及以上弱视筛查线 0.80，建议医师复核",
      "本单为散瞳验光，瞳孔恢复后度数可能略有变化，建议复验后配镜"
    ],
    "must_read": [
      "儿童青少年首选离焦镜片以延缓近视进展",
      "首次配镜建议 3 个月内复查视力与戴镜适应情况"
    ],
    "generated_at": "2026-10-31T10:02:00+08:00",
    "cached": false
  },
  "trace_id": "b7e2a1c94f3d5e80"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `X-Internal-Token`；验光单必须存在且 `status IN ('SIGNED','ARCHIVED')`，`DRAFT` 返回 `"1001037"`，`VOID` 返回 `"1001001"`。
2. **取数优先级：** `ACCEPTED` > `FINAL` > `RAW`；均不存在返回 `"1001009"`。`source_stage` 明确返回所用阶段。
3. **处方调整规则（`adjust`，均落 `adjust_note` 说明）：**
   - 散光柱镜 ≤ −0.25D 且患者年龄 ≥ 40：柱镜可减 0.25D（提高适应性）
   - 双眼 SE 差 ≥ 2.00D：低度眼可适度欠矫（减 0.25~0.50D），`recommendation_level = 'CAUTION'`
   - 高度近视（SE ≤ −6.00D）：球镜一般不全矫（减 0.25~0.50D）
   - 6 岁以下首次配镜：**不做减配**，足矫优先保证视觉发育
4. **镜片类型建议（`suggested_lens_type`）：**
   - 年龄 < 18 且近视 → `DEFOCUS`（离焦，近视防控优先）
   - 存在 `ADD` 需求或年龄 ≥ 40 且有近用需求 → `PROGRESSIVE`
   - 存在棱镜 → `PRISM`
   - 其余 → `SINGLE_VISION`
   - `lens_preference` 参数可由 M10-02 覆盖（`DEFOCUS`/`PROGRESSIVE`/`SINGLE_VISION`）
5. **建议强度（`recommendation_level`）：**
   - `STRONG`：数据完整、无异常、非散瞳或散瞳已复验
   - `NORMAL`：数据完整但存在轻微异常（如视功能轻度异常）
   - `CAUTION`：存在弱视疑似、高度屈光参差、散瞳未复验、儿童未散瞳等需医师判断的情况
6. **风险提示（`warnings`）：** 弱视疑似、散瞳未复验、高度数、屈光参差、视功能异常、OK 镜配戴中。
7. **必读说明（`must_read`）：** 按年龄与度数生成的配镜注意事项。
8. 瞳距/瞳高从 `m10_optometry_vision` 取（`PD`/`PH` 行）；缺失时返回 null 并在 `warnings` 提示「瞳距未测，开方前需补测」。
9. 结果缓存 Redis `m10:optometry:p03:{optometry_no}:{lens_pref}` TTL 300s。
10. **本接口只给建议，不生成处方号、不落库、不涉及任何商品与价格**——正式处方由 M10-02 开立。

**调用方与 SLA：**

| 调用方 | 用途 | SLA |
|---|---|---|
| M10-02 | 开方页加载时取验光数据与建议 | P95 ≤ 150ms |

```json
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. 校验 `X-Internal-Token` 与 `Idempotency-Key`。
2. 必填校验：`exam_task_id`（正整数）与 `exam_code` 缺失返回 `"1001036"`。
3. **幂等：** 按 `uk_m10_optometry_task`（`exam_task_id` WHERE 非作废未删除）查重；已存在则返回 `is_duplicate=true` + 现有 `optometry_id` + `"1001035"` 语义（HTTP 200，`code=0`，`data.is_duplicate=true`）。
4. 检查项目映射：仅 `EXAM_REFRACTION`（验光）、`EXAM_VA`（视力）、`EXAM_IOP`（眼压）三类触发建单；其他 `exam_code` 返回 `accepted=false` 并说明「该项目不生成验光单」（不报错）。
5. 患者校验：`patient_id` 为空时返回 `"1001006"`。
6. 建草稿：`status='DRAFT'`、`source_system='M10'`、`source_report_no`、`exam_task_id`、`patient_id`、`patient_name`、`dept_id`、`exam_date = completed_at::date`。
7. `result_summary` 存在时预填 `m10_optometry_device_sync`（`sync_channel='API'`）+ 解析结果，暂不回写屈光明细（需验光师确认）。
8. 生成排队号；写日志 `CREATE`（`operator_role='SYSTEM'`，`operator_id=0`）。
9. 发事件 `m10.optometry.created`（草稿事件，通知 M10-05 前台工作台刷新）。
10. 返回 `optometry_no=null`（草稿无号）。

**调用方与 SLA：**

| 调用方 | 用途 | SLA |
|---|---|---|
| M12 | 技师完成验光/视力/眼压检查后通知 M10 生成待验光 | P95 ≤ 100ms |

### 6.4 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/v1/m10/optometry.py
"""M10-01 验光与视功能检查 —— 对外路由层"""

from datetime import date
from typing import List, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Body, Depends, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import PageResult, success_response
from app.core.security import get_current_user, require_permission
from app.schemas.m10.optometry import (
    DeviceImportIn, OnExamCompletedIn, OptometryCreateIn, OptometryQueryIn,
    OptometrySignIn, OptometryUpdateIn, OptometryVoidIn, RefractionRowIn,
    VisionRowIn, VisualFuncRowIn,
)
from app.services.m10.optometry_service import OptometryService
from app.services.m10.optometry_calc_service import OptometryCalcService
from app.services.m10.optometry_device_service import OptometryDeviceService
from app.services.m10.optometry_sign_service import OptometrySignService

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m10/optometry", tags=["M10-01 验光与视功能检查"])
internal_router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m10/optometry", tags=["M10-01 内部契约"])


# ==================== 工作台与看板 ====================

@router.get("/workbench", summary="验光工作站首页概览")
async def get_workbench(
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:optometry:view")),
):
    """返回今日概览、我的草稿、质量提醒与待办数。"""
    svc = OptometryService(db)
    data = await svc.get_workbench(operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/workbench/my-drafts", summary="我的进行中验光")
async def list_my_drafts(
    limit: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=50),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:optometry:view")),
):
    """查询当前用户未完成的验光草稿。"""
    svc = OptometryService(db)
    items = await svc.list_my_drafts(user.id, limit)
    return success_response({"items": items, "total": len(items)})


@router.get("/workbench/quality-alerts", summary="质量提醒清单")
async def list_quality_alerts(
    alert_type: str = Query("DOUBLE_CHECK_PENDING"),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:optometry:view")),
):
    """质量提醒四类：手工未核对 / 设备偏离 / 儿童未散瞳 / 草稿将过期。"""
    svc = OptometryService(db)
    items, total = await svc.list_quality_alerts(
        alert_type, page, page_size, operator=user)
    return success_response(PageResult(
        items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,
        pages=(total + page_size - 1) // page_size,
    ))


@router.get("/workbench/statistics", summary="个人绩效概览")
async def get_my_statistics(
    period: str = Query("WEEK", pattern="^(TODAY|WEEK|MONTH|QUARTER|YEAR)$"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:optometry:view")),
):
    """当前用户区间绩效：签发量、作废率、平均耗时、核对率。"""
    svc = OptometryService(db)
    data = await svc.get_personal_statistics(user.id, period)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/options", summary="下拉选项")
async def list_options(
    types: str = Query("optometry_type,cycloplegic_agent,device"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
):
    """登录即可：验光类型/散瞳药物/设备等枚举。"""
    svc = OptometryService(db)
    data = await svc.get_options(types.split(","))
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== 排队与叫号 ====================

@router.get("/queue", summary="验光排队队列")
async def get_queue(
    org_id: int = Query(1),
    room_no: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=8),
    mode: str = Query("workstation", pattern="^(workstation|display)$"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:optometry:view")),
):
    """返回当前叫号、等待队列与散瞳观察区；display 模式脱敏。"""

    data = await svc.get_queue(org_id, room_no, mode)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/queue/{optometry_id}/call", summary="叫号")
async def call_patient(
    optometry_id: int,
    payload: dict = Body(default_factory=dict),
    request: Request = None,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:optometry:create")),
):
    """设置当前叫号并推送大屏；同一验光室不可并叫。"""
    svc = OptometryService(db)
    data = await svc.call_patient(
        optometry_id, payload, operator=user,
        idem_key=request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key"))
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/queue/{optometry_id}/skip", summary="过号")
async def skip_patient(
    optometry_id: int,

    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),

):

    svc = OptometryService(db)
    data = await svc.skip_patient(optometry_id, payload, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)

):

    svc = OptometryService(db)

    return success_response(data)

async def create_optometry(

):

async def update_optometry(

):

    svc = OptometryService(db)
    data = await svc.save(

        idem_key=request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key"))
    return success_response(data)

    return success_response(data)


@router.delete("/{optometry_id}", summary="放弃草稿")
async def discard_draft(

):

@router.post("/{optometry_id}/lock", summary="获取编辑锁")
async def acquire_lock(
    optometry_id: int,
    payload: dict = Body(default_factory=dict),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:optometry:update")),
):
    """Redis 编辑锁，TTL 60s，主管可强制抢占。"""

    data = await svc.acquire_edit_lock(
        optometry_id, bool(payload.get("force", False)), operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.delete("/{optometry_id}/lock", summary="释放编辑锁")

):

    customer_id: Optional[int] = Query(None),
    optometrist_id: Optional[int] = Query(None),
    optometry_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    status: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    is_cycloplegic: Optional[bool] = Query(None),
    is_amblyopia_suspect: Optional[bool] = Query(None),
    is_progression_alert: Optional[bool] = Query(None),
    date_from: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    date_to: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:optometry:view")),
):
    """多条件组合查询，日期跨度 ≤731 天。"""
    svc = OptometryService(db)
    q = OptometryQueryIn(
        keyword=keyword, patient_id=patient_id, customer_id=customer_id,
        optometrist_id=optometrist_id,
        optometry_type=optometry_type.split(",") if optometry_type else None,
        status=status.split(",") if status else None,
        is_cycloplegic=is_cycloplegic,
        is_amblyopia_suspect=is_amblyopia_suspect,
        is_progression_alert=is_progression_alert,
        date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to, page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    items, total = await svc.list_optometry(q, operator=user)
    return success_response(PageResult(
        items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,
        pages=(total + page_size - 1) // page_size,
    ))


@router.get("/{optometry_id}/history-trend", summary="患者验光历史趋势")
async def get_history_trend(
    optometry_id: int,
    limit: int = Query(5, ge=2, le=20),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:optometry:view")),
):
    """取最近 N 次已签发单的 SE 曲线与预警点。"""
    svc = OptometryCalcService(db)
    data = await svc.get_history_trend(optometry_id, limit)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/{optometry_id}/logs", summary="验光单操作时间线")
async def get_optometry_logs(
    optometry_id: int,
    limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200),
    include_diff: bool = Query(False),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:optometry:view")),
):
    """只增日志的只读视图。"""
    svc = OptometryService(db)
    items = await svc.list_logs(optometry_id, limit, include_diff, operator=user)
    return success_response({"items": items, "total": len(items)})


@router.get("/patient/{patient_id}/timeline", summary="患者全部验光时间线")
async def get_patient_timeline(
    patient_id: int,
    date_from: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:optometry:view")),
):
    """含作废单（标注状态）与关联处方/订单计数。"""
    svc = OptometryService(db)
    items, total = await svc.get_patient_timeline(
        patient_id, date_from, page, page_size, operator=user)
    return success_response(PageResult(
        items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,
        pages=(total + page_size - 1) // page_size,
    ))

@router.get("/statistics/summary", summary="工作量与质量统计")
async def get_statistics_summary(
    date_from: date = Query(...),
    date_to: date = Query(...),
    group_by: str = Query("OPTOMETRIST", pattern="^(OPTOMETRIST|DEPT|TYPE|HOUR)$"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:optometry:stat")),
):

    data = await svc.get_statistics_summary(date_from, date_to, group_by, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/statistics/detail", summary="按验光师明细下钻")
async def get_statistics_detail(

    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),

):

    data = await svc.get_statistics_detail(
        optometrist_id, date_from, date_to, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/statistics/export", summary="统计导出（异步）")
async def export_statistics(

):

        payload, operator=user,
        idem_key=request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key"))
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/config", summary="查询验光参数配置")
async def get_config(
    scope: Optional[str] = Query(None, pattern="^(GLOBAL|DEPT)$"),
    scope_id: Optional[int] = Query(None),
    param_group: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:optometry:view")),
):

    data = await svc.get_config(scope, scope_id, param_group)
    return success_response(data)


@router.put("/config", summary="修改验光参数配置")

):

    svc = OptometryService(db)
    data = await svc.update_config(items, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)
```

```python
# app/api/internal/m10/optometry.py
"""M10-01 验光与视功能检查 —— 内部契约路由（P-01 ~ P-04）"""

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import success_response
from app.core.internal_auth import verify_internal_token
from app.schemas.m10.optometry import OnExamCompletedIn
from app.services.m10.optometry_service import OptometryService
from app.services.m10.optometry_prescription_service import (
    PrescriptionSuggestionService,
)

internal_router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/internal/v1/m10/optometry",
    tags=["M10-01 内部契约"],
    dependencies=[Depends(verify_internal_token)],
)


@internal_router.get("/{optometry_no}", summary="P-01 按验光单号取验光结果")
async def internal_get_optometry(
    optometry_no: str,
    include_visual_func: bool = Query(False),
    x_internal_token: str = Header(default=""),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """M16-04 U-04 / M07 / M11 调用。

    只返回已签发（SIGNED）或已归档（ARCHIVED）的验光单；
    草稿返回 "1001002"，已作废与不存在均返回 "1001001"。
    """
    svc = OptometryService(db)
    data = await svc.get_signed_result(optometry_no, include_visual_func)
    return success_response(data)


@internal_router.get(
    "/by-patient/{patient_id}/latest", summary="P-02 患者最近一次验光")
async def internal_latest_by_patient(
    patient_id: int,
    max_age_days: int = Query(365, ge=1, le=3650),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """M07 / M11 / M16-04 调用；无记录返回 found=false 与 "1001038"。"""
    svc = OptometryService(db)
    data = await svc.get_latest_by_patient(patient_id, max_age_days)
    return success_response(data)


@internal_router.get(
    "/{optometry_no}/prescription-suggestion",
    summary="P-03 由验光单生成处方建议")
async def internal_prescription_suggestion(
    optometry_no: str,
    lens_preference: str = Query(
        "AUTO", pattern="^(AUTO|SINGLE_VISION|PROGRESSIVE|DEFOCUS|PRISM)$"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """M10-02 调用；只给建议，不生成处方、不涉价格。"""
    svc = PrescriptionSuggestionService(db)
    data = await svc.build(optometry_no, lens_preference)
    return success_response(data)


@internal_router.post("/on-exam-completed", summary="P-04 检查完成通知")
async def internal_on_exam_completed(
    payload: OnExamCompletedIn,
    request: Request,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """M12 调用；按 exam_task_id 幂等生成待验光草稿。"""
    svc = OptometryService(db)
    data = await svc.on_exam_completed(
        payload, idem_key=request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key"))
    return success_response(data)
```
